  "meta_commentary": "Sprint 1 is pure triage/setup \u2014 zero reigh-worker runtime code changes, so there is no pytest to run. The \\\"tests\\\" are the four Python asserts in T6, which are the authoritative acceptance gates. Execute strictly in order: T1 (tag) gives the rollback anchor and MUST land first; T2 (fork) and T3 (clone+SHA alignment) are sequential but cheap; T4 (walker) produces the coverage target that T5 consumes; T5 (CSV) is the bulk deliverable; T6 gates sprint completion.\\n\\nThe two highest-risk ways this sprint fails silently: (1) misclassifying a banodoco-specific hunk as `(a) accept-upstream` \u2014 if unsure about a non-ltx2 file, prefer `(b)` or `(c)` and flag, don't guess `(a)`; (2) a CSV path that doesn't byte-match a walker path \u2014 the set-based coverage gate will fail loudly, but it's easy to introduce via path normalization. Treat `/tmp/wan2gp-differ.paths` as the source of truth and copy paths verbatim.\\n\\nThe pre-classified clear_conditioning row is frozen in shape \u2014 the strings in T5's verbatim row must appear exactly. If tools.py has additional CONTENT_DIFF beyond clear_conditioning, merge notes into the same row's `hunk` field so bucket/carry_location/verification stay unchanged. A second row for tools.py is only warranted if the additional drift needs a different owner or verification \u2014 unlikely, but allowed by SD-002 (which names the column `hunk`, plural-allowed).\\n\\nFork-location deviation is expected to be common: `banodoco` org membership may not permit forking via the API even though peteromallet has `read:org`. If the fork fails, capture stderr verbatim and fall back to `peteromallet/Wan2GP` without stopping \u2014 this is the brief's documented path. Sprint 2's submodule URL will consume whichever location was resolved.\\n\\nFLAG-002 remains open (minor): fork and clone SHAs are recorded separately. T3 adds a lightweight alignment probe, but the CSV's authoritative SHA is the clone's, so Sprint 2 must reconcile at rebase time. Note this in executor_notes rather than trying to fix it with extra tooling this sprint.\\n\\nThe debt watch items in this session are about worker-process lifecycle (worker sigterm / supervisor invocation / startup window) \u2014 they are entirely unrelated to this sprint's surface area. The no-reigh-worker-code-changes constraint already protects against accidentally regressing them. If a walker happens to surface something adjacent to `source/task_handlers/` or `source/runtime/worker/`, confirm the sprint surface is still Wan2GP/ only and do not expand scope.\\n\\nFinally: the tag is LOCAL-ONLY. Do not push it, do not push branches, do not open any PR in this sprint. The only artifacts that escape the local machine are the fork (via `gh`) and optionally the clone target in `/tmp/`.",

  "cmd": "python3 - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport hashlib\n\nvendored = Path([REDACTED])\nupstream = Path('/tmp/wan2gp-upstream')\nout_path = Path('/tmp/wan2gp-differ.paths')\n\nskip_dirs = {'__pycache__', '.git'}\nskip_suffixes = {'.pyc'}\n\ndef walk(root: Path):\n    files = {}\n    for path in root.rglob('*'):\n        rel = path.relative_to(root)\n        if any(part in skip_dirs for part in rel.parts):\n            continue\n        if path.is_dir():\n            continue\n        if path.suffix in skip_suffixes:\n            continue\n        files[rel.as_posix()] = path\n    return files\n\ndef sha256(path: Path):\n    h = hashlib.sha256()\n    with path.open('rb') as f:\n        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b''):\n            h.update(chunk)\n    return h.hexdigest()\n\nvendored_files = walk(vendored)\nupstream_files = walk(upstream)\nall_paths = sorted(set(vendored_files) | set(upstream_files))\nstatus_counts = {'VENDORED_ONLY': 0, 'UPSTREAM_ONLY': 0, 'CONTENT_DIFF': 0}\ndiffering = []\nfor rel in all_paths:\n    vp = vendored_files.get(rel)\n    up = upstream_files.get(rel)\n    if vp is None:\n        status_counts['UPSTREAM_ONLY'] += 1\n        differing.append((rel, 'UPSTREAM_ONLY'))\n    elif up is None:\n        status_counts['VENDORED_ONLY'] += 1\n        differing.append((rel, 'VENDORED_ONLY'))\n    else:\n        if sha256(vp) != sha256(up):\n            status_counts['CONTENT_DIFF'] += 1\n            differing.append((rel, 'CONTENT_DIFF'))\n\nwith out_path.open('w', encoding='utf-8', newline='') as f:\n    for rel, _status in differing:\n        f.write(f'Wan2GP/{rel}\\n')\n\nprint(f\"wrote={out_path}\")\nprint(f\"total={len(differing)}\")\nfor key in ('VENDORED_ONLY', 'UPSTREAM_ONLY', 'CONTENT_DIFF'):\n    print(f\"{key}={status_counts[key]}\")\nPY",
